## HOSPEDAJE Y VUELO · US-11 + US-12 + US-13 (REVIEW MODE)
458. **ONE CANONICAL RECORD PER FOLIO** (`SMV_HV.RECS` + session `hv.recs`; `hvRecs/hvOf/hvPut`): `hos[]` = one lodging request per destination with nights (`via` HOS unit / HOT external hotel; HOS estado pendAut · aprobada · rechazada · cancelada; `hot` estado enProceso · asignado · cancelado · rechazadoVia, causa politica | rechazoHSM) · `vue` (estado pendAsig · asignado · rechazado · pagado · confirmado · rechazadoVia; `rondas[]` append-only, each with `ops[]`, `vigencia`, optional `rechazo`; `sel`, `compra`, `confirm`) · `lockVia` (Viáticos). Never copies the trip: dates, destinations, medio and lodging type come from the approved request (`svAllReqs`, seeds 00091–00100 in the same request model). History reconstructed from the record (`hvHist`); every transition also writes Bitácora (areas Hospedaje · Hospedaje HSM · Hotel externo · Vuelos · Presupuesto; birth-date consultation = «Dato sensible»).
459. **STAGE ENABLED ONLY WITH SOL_Aprobada** (`hvElig`): pendiente/captura → «Etapa bloqueada» explained («tu viaje está pendiente de autorización»); rechazada/cancelada → No aplica. Dates read-only («Fechas del viaje» card). Hospedaje/Vuelo stay SUBPROCESSES of the folio (rule 5): the 4 canonical stages are untouched.
460. **LODGING ROUTE FROM THE APPROVED PLANNING + PROFILE**: segment `hospedaje = hospital` (destination is a unit) → HOS to the Hotelería of THAT unit with legend «según tu perfil, se prioriza hospedaje en unidad hospitalaria»; `hotel` (Corporativo, profile with hotel, motivo) → HOT directly to Viáticos, chip «Incluido por política», no unit priority shown. Room type from the profile (`hvHab`). 72 h / 24 h (`PARAMS.antelacionHoras / cancelacionHoras`) shown as support text from the start; < 72 h ⇒ «caso urgente» (sent with `urgente`, highlighted to Hotelería). Traveler cancels ≥ 24 h before entry (with motive); after that → Viáticos.
461. **FLIGHT ONLY IF THE PLANNING CHOSE AVIÓN** (`hvVueApplies`): otherwise «NO APLICA» with origin, destinations and medio (explained, not hidden). Tipo de vuelo (ida y regreso / solo ida), horas aproximadas and equipaje are NOT asked again: they were captured in Creación per destination (`segmentos[].vuelo`) and are shown read-only («De tu solicitud aprobada», `hvPlanVue`); the traveler only completes the passenger data Creación does not ask — fecha de nacimiento (shared date field) + nacionalidad (advance notice if missing) → «Solicitar vuelo» → VUE_Pendiente asignación (manager 2026-10-16). «Solo regreso» stays a backend value only (Creación offers ida y regreso / solo ida). Options as comparable cards (radio, stacked on phone, confirm button at the foot); choose exactly one (definitive, confirmation before → after) or «Ninguna me sirve» with motive (catalog + text) → VUE_Rechazado; Viáticos publishes a NEW round on the same case, previous rounds kept read-only.
462. **HOTELERÍA HSM** (`res-hsm`, HH ✓): strictly its own unit (`user.hospital`); no amounts, budget or bank data. Tabs Por atender (counter = not taken by others) · Confirmadas · Rechazadas · Canceladas (counter = constancia pending); sum in the menu (rule 418). Take/release (lock with holder + time; others read-only). Confirm requires room number with LIVE conflict detection (same room, overlapping dates vs `OCUPACION` + approved stays) and the profile room type next to the field → HOS_Aprobada, no budget movement. Reject requires motive (catalog + text) with the consequence shown BEFORE → HOS_Rechazada + automatic HOT_En proceso case in Viáticos (causal link, Bitácora «Solicitud de habitación rechazada · sin disponibilidad · pasa a Viáticos»). Room change while the trip has not started, audited anterior → nuevo. Cancellations reflected with «Registrar enterado y liberar habitación». Permanent panel «Política de canalización entre unidades cercanas» (HIP ↔ ROM · TLN ↔ LPA).
463. **VIÁTICOS** (`res-hotel`, VIA ✓): tabs Hotel externo · Vuelos (counters = actionable) · Concluidos; folio lock «Atiende: nombre» (concurrency); own folio read-only. Hotel: catalog of convenio hotels by destination or «Otro hotel» with justification; dirección, tipo de habitación, desayuno (reminder «solo podrá comprobar comida y cena»), tarifa with the PROFILE TOPE next to it (excess detected while typing ⇒ required justification, recorded as «Excepción a la política»), documento de reserva; budget impact shown BEFORE the button; confirm ⇒ HOT_Asignado + «Reserva» row for the amount not already reserved (rechazoHSM: full tarifa × noches; política: difference over the folio's Hospedaje estimate). Flight: birth date visible to Viáticos only (consultation logged as Dato sensible), build ≥ 2 options, publish (VUE_Asignado, vigencia), buy (comprobante; «Vuelo» row en tránsito in Movimientos de bolsa + Reserva for the difference over the estimated Vuelos) ⇒ VUE_Pagado, confirm (clave + itinerario PDF) ⇒ VUE_Confirmado + bell notice with the itinerary. Insufficient budget ⇒ blocked with route to the Monitor (supplements only by file load, rule 419); MODO DEMO «Presupuesto al confirmar hotel o vuelo». Viáticos may reject any process (motive).
464. **SAME JOURNEY / HOME / MIS VIAJES / BELL**: `hvTodo` drives the traveler CTA («Confirmar hospedaje» · «Configurar vuelo» · «Elegir vuelo», before the anticipo CTA), the «Qué sigue» text («Viaje aprobado. Confirma hospedaje y vuelo.»), the menu counters and «Requieren mi acción»; Mis viajes Hospedaje / Vuelo sections read the same record (`hvMvSec`) and link to the module with the folio. **Traveler navigation = ONE option «Planeación de viaje › Hotel y vuelos»** (manager 2026-10-16; `hospedaje` route, `vuelo` = alias that opens the Vuelos tab) with the concepts SEPARATED in tabs **Hospedaje · Vuelos** (same pattern as Viáticos' «Hotel externo · Vuelos»; manager correction 2026-10-16): each tab has its own list («Hospedaje de mis viajes»: tipo de hospedaje · noches · estatus · «Vuelos de mis viajes»: ruta · configuración · estatus), its own counter (sum in the menu, rule 418) and its own detail («Detalle del hospedaje» / «Detalle del vuelo»: fechas + only that concept + its confirmations + its history). **The shared journey component** (Inicio «Tu viaje actual» + Detalle del viaje, `journey({ folio })` → `hvJourneySub`) shows a «Hotel y vuelos» strip under the 4 canonical stages (stages unchanged, rule 5) with Hospedaje / Vuelo status chips (each chip opens THAT concept's detail for the folio) + «Ver hotel y vuelos» (opens the module list, on the tab with the pending action); it reads the canonical record, so every transition (traveler, Hotelería, Viáticos) is reflected in Inicio, Detalle, Mis viajes sections, list, menu counter and bell at once. Home Hotelería tray + KPI «Solicitudes pendientes» and Home Viáticos tray «Hotel externo y vuelos» + KPIs Hotel externo / Vuelos come from the same record (static demo rows replaced, never mixed). Capital Humano HSM operates the same screen for its externos («Capturando para …»; notices to the registrador). Downloads (confirmación de hospedaje, itinerario) open in the shared document viewer, listed ONCE in «Confirmaciones» (rule 445).

### HOSPEDAJE Y VUELO · PENDIENTES (client questions, not rules)
- Official names/codes of ROM, TLN, LPA and whether canalization between nearby units is automatic or a Hotelería decision (prototype: panel + text only; HSM Chihuahua has no nearby unit).
- Hotelería Corporativo Home still shows a static tray «Rechazadas por Hotelería HSM» targeting Hotel y vuelos; the story says Hotelería Corporativo does not attend it — confirm removal.
- Vigencia of flight options (demo 72 h), minimum options (demo 2) and whether Viáticos may extend vigencia.
- Tope used for the hotel: prototype = profile c1 tariff; exceptions (r3) are not yet read here.
- Budget for flights above the estimated Vuelos reference and hotel by policy above the Hospedaje estimate (difference committed) — confirm; seed folios 00091–00100 have no seed Reserva rows.
- Cancellation of hotel/flight after purchase = US-16 (not built); traveler cancellation < 24 h goes to Viáticos.
- Whether Viáticos rejection of a lodging/flight request needs a catalog; whether the traveler can resubmit after it.

## Status (2026-10-14 · ANTICIPO · US-14 + US-15)
**US-14 — Solicitud de anticipo (monto propuesto por el sistema) · REVIEW MODE · SYNC PENDING** · **US-15 — Anticipo: autorización, contabilización, transferencia y depósito · REVIEW MODE · SYNC PENDING** (prototype only; no PDF / standalone / ZIP). US-06, US-07, US-09 stay REVIEW MODE · SYNC PENDING (consumed, not modified).

## ANTICIPO · US-14 + US-15 (REVIEW MODE)
436. **ONE CANONICAL ANTICIPO RECORD PER FOLIO** (`us14-data.js › SMV_ANT.ANTICIPOS` + session `an.recs`; `antRecs/antOf`): US-14 creates it, US-15 operates it; never copies the trip (reads `svAllReqs()`). Fields: folio · uid · estado · propuesta (FROZEN at submit) · sol / aprobado (per concept) · declaración · pago (SAP, masked) · cadenaIds + decisiones (append-only, one per step) · lock · contab (póliza) · pp (propuesta de pago) · transfers[] (incl. second transfer US-16 as ADJUSTMENT of the same record) · motivo. Estados: pendAut · autorizado · contabilizado · porTransferir · realizado (ANT_Realizado) · devuelto (reabierto) · rechazado · cancelado. «En proceso» = capture (not persisted). History is RECONSTRUCTED from the record (`antHist`); every transition also writes Bitácora (`antEv`, areas Anticipo / Autorización / Contabilización / Presupuesto).
437. **ELIGIBILITY = one resolver `antElig(r)`** (screen + «server»): externo → «No aplica · viajero externo» (no record possible) · perfil sin anticipo / Director E-ED (`anticipoNA`, rule 253) → No aplica + «Ir a Comprobación» · trip not yet approved → «Después de la aprobación» · open record → existing (one open per trip, `PARAMS.abiertos`) · sin tarifa «Anticipo · monto máximo autorizado» → blocked · viaje iniciado/concluido → «Viaje vencido» · < 5 días de antelación (`PARAMS.antelacionDias`, distinct from the 4 days of the travel request) → blocked · cuenta SAP no vigente → blocked with update route. Traveler profile decides eligibility/policy; functional role never.
438. **SYSTEM PROPOSAL = `antPropuesta(r)`** from the folio's destinations and EFFECTIVE values (excepción individual → política del perfil → parámetro global): Hospedaje (hotel nights × c1 / r3) · Alimentos (days × daily cap by number of external meals, c2 / r2) · Tintorería (global) · Gasolina / Casetas (auto particular, c7/c8) · Taxi (weekly tariff by destination hospital × weeks, editable) · Autobús / Vuelos shown as $0 with reason. Total = min(Σ concepts, tope) where tope = r14 exception (NEW rule in REGLAS_EXC, demo e6 u09) or tariff c4; if Σ > tope, concepts are scaled proportionally and marked. Origin chip per concept + «Consultar el detalle del cálculo».
439. **REQUESTED ≤ PROPOSED (hard)**: only `PARAMS.editables` (Taxi, Autobús) accept a LOWER amount; per-concept error under the field with the maximum and its origin; total ≤ proposal; declaración obligatoria; validated again in `antSubmit` (owner only, idempotent `_antBusy`). Requesting less is valid and labeled as such. «Monto propuesto por el sistema» and «Monto de anticipo solicitado» are separate lines.
440. **AUTHORIZATION CHAIN = `antChain(uid)`** (US-15: Director inmediato → Dirección Corporativa, SAP structure; NOT the US-09 travel chain; no DGA/CH CO steps; duplicates/self removed). Tray **Autorización › Anticipos** (`aut-ant`, DIR · CHC · DGA ✓, actions CO + AU) with Por autorizar / Resueltos por mí; population = signer in turn (`antPending`) feeds tab counter, sidebar sum and bell «Requieren mi acción». Reject = catalog (`MOTIVOS_RECHAZO`) + text (required for «Otro motivo»), visible to the traveler.
441. **CONTABILIDAD = Contabilización › Solicitud de anticipo** (`con-ant`, CON; actions CO · VA Validar = tomar, contabilizar, rechazar/cancelar · **PG Procesar pago** NEW = propuesta de pago, confirmar transferencia, devolución). Tabs: Por contabilizar · Propuesta de pago · Por transferir · Realizados · Rechazados y cancelados · En autorización (read-only); counters on the first three = sidebar sum. Take (lock; other analysts read-only) → contabilizar (approved per concept ≤ solicitado, justification required if it differs, visible to the collaborator; póliza; once) → propuesta de pago grouped by sociedad + banco (accounts must be vigentes; export CSV) → confirmar depósito (fecha no futura + referencia; individual or batch with consecutive references; importe distinto ⇒ justificación) → ANT_Realizado + traveler notice (monto, cuenta, plazo desde el fin del viaje). Devolución bancaria → devuelto (causa catalog) → reincluir en propuesta. Second transfer (US-16) = adjustment row of the same anticipo. MODO DEMO «Condición del anticipo»: cuenta no vigente · viaje vencido · falla de integración con SAP (nothing applied, «Reintentar»).
442. **BUDGET = CONSUMED** (rule 419 semantics unchanged; Movimientos presupuestales still Carga inicial · Suplemento · Bloqueo): the approved folio is already Comprometido (Reserva). At the LAST authorization only the difference above the folio's net reserve is committed (Reserva «compromiso adicional del anticipo»; e.g. Taxi is not in the travel estimate). Confirmed deposit = ONE «Anticipo» row in Movimientos de bolsa (→ En tránsito); devolución = negative «Anticipo» row; contab rejection/cancel releases only the additional commitment. Seed folios get their Reserva / Anticipo rows from `antSeedMovs()` (injected in `presData`). The traveler never sees entity figures.
443. **SAME JOURNEY / HOME / MIS VIAJES**: stage 2 from `antStage(r)` (Disponible para solicitar · Pendiente autorización nivel n de m — firmante · Autorizado · en contabilización · Transferencia en proceso · Depositado fecha · monto · Transferencia devuelta · Rechazado (red) · No aplica); CTA `antCta` = SOLICITAR ANTICIPO / VER ANTICIPO (same folio); Mis viajes «Anticipo» section `antSec` + «Qué sigue» `antNext`; Home Contabilidad tray «Anticipos autorizados» + KPI «Anticipos por transferir» from the same records. Companion = own folio, own proposal, own anticipo (origin untouched).

444. **ORDEN DE BOTONES EN UNA FILA (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-14)**: the main action is always the FIRST one the user reads. Right-aligned action bars and modal footers (decision bars, sticky bars, forms) put the primary at the far RIGHT and the secondary/destructive to its left (`row-reverse` when the list is built primary-first); left-aligned card actions put the primary at the far LEFT; phone (stacked) = primary on TOP. Applied: US-09 decision bar (AUTORIZAR right of RECHAZAR), Anticipo action bar (traveler · autorizador · Contabilidad); modals and Externos form already complied.

445. **UN DOCUMENTO = UN TÍTULO, SIN REPETIRLO (2026-10-14)**: Creación pide UN solo documento para todos los excedentes del perfil (`docsReq.limite`), titulado «Autorización del excedente» en Creación, Datos del viaje › Documentos y el detalle de autorización. El detalle de autorización YA NO tiene tarjeta «Desviaciones y evidencia» (manager 2026-10-14): los documentos (evidencia adicional, autorización del excedente, preautorización de DGA) se consultan solo en Datos del viaje › Documentos. Si el cliente pidiera un documento por excepción, cada desviación tendría su propio documento y título (pendiente de confirmar).

446. **EXCEPCIÓN MARCADA EN SU CAMPO (2026-10-14)**: if a request carries excesos (registered by Creación) the approval detail tags the field that originates each one with the amber chip «Excepción a la política» + the standard «?» help (hover/focus/click popover, `helpTip`) carrying the reason — never loose inline text (`apExcInfo`): Hospedaje of every destination with hotel externo · Fechas del viaje for días por viaje / noches por semana / semanas con viaje en el mes. The single document «Autorización del excedente» in Documentos lists what it covers («Cubre las n excepciones: …»). No separate deviations card (rule 445). **Trip-level exceptions** (one value for the whole trip, not per destination — e.g. Gasolina / Casetas over the profile's per-trip maximum, or any other global concept) are tagged with the same chip + «?» help on their row of «Montos estimados vs tarifas del perfil» (right of the concept, row height unchanged); per-destination exceptions stay on the destination field. Recorded 2026-10-14 (manager note) — the demo has no global-concept exception yet; implement with the first case that Creación registers.

457. **SIN «X» DE CIERRE CUANDO EL MODAL YA OFRECE CÓMO SALIR (permanent, global UI rule, client 2026-10-16)**: a modal that has its own dismiss/cancel button (Volver · Cancelar · Cerrar) shows NO close «X» in its header; that button, Escape and the overlay click close it. The «X» stays only where there is no footer button to leave (visor de documentos, drawer móvil). Applied: diálogos US-26 (`closeModal`), selector de hospitales, decisión de autorización (`apm`), detalle del movimiento (`pmMov`), confirmaciones (`closeConfirm`).

448. **BOTONES RELLENOS SIN CONTORNO DE FOCO (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-15)**: filled buttons (primary blue #00A7E1, destructive solid #F25757) never draw an outline or ring in ANY focus state — mouse click, keyboard, or the programmatic autofocus of modals (success CONTINUAR, confirmations). One shared CSS rule (`!important`, wins over the generic `button:focus-visible`); outlined/secondary buttons keep their keyboard-only focus outline.

**PDF NOTE (client 2026-10-16, documentation only — the mockup is NOT changed yet)**: in the next PDF that documents Mis viajes (US-14/US-15 package; US-10 stays closed), state as a CONFIRMED client rule: in Mis viajes the amount data (Montos estimados / anticipo por concepto) do NOT list concepts whose amount is $0 — only concepts with an amount greater than $0 appear (e.g. Vuelos $0, Autobús $0 are omitted).

### ANTICIPO · PENDIENTES (client questions, not rules)
- US-14 says «DGA sí tiene anticipo», but the approved model makes the DGA holder travel with perfil Director grupo ED (No aplica). Prototype keeps the approved model.
- Taxi weekly proration (prototype: ⌈días/7⌉ semanas) and whether Taxi belongs in the advance at all (client earlier: taxi only in comprobaciones); Autobús has no tariff.
- Whether hotel externo lodging is paid by the traveler from the advance or directly by Viáticos (prototype includes it, per the story example).
- Director tariff c4 is null (Directors not E/ED cannot request: «Sin tarifa vigente»).
- Budget: anticipo may exceed the folio reserve (difference committed at authorization) — confirm; rule-419 reconciliation assumes the reserve stops being vigente once an anticipo exists.
- New request after a REJECTED anticipo (prototype: no; only after a cancellation).
- Who else can be «Director inmediato» (CTR/CON heads in SAP) — `aut-ant` visible only to DIR · CHC · DGA.
- Official catalogs (rechazo, contabilidad, causas de devolución), bank per collaborator (SAP), propuesta de pago export format, SAP integration contract.

## US-09 · APROBACIÓN DE VIAJE (APPROVED · CLOSED 2026-10-16)
423. **SAME REQUESTS AS CREACIÓN**: the tray, Home, bell, Mis viajes and Monitor read `svAllReqs()` (US-08 seed + `us09-data.js` seed + session). Approval never copies a request; decisions are appended to the folio (`decisiones[]`: paso · res · motivoTipo · motivo · byId · when · canal), append-only, one valid decision per step (idempotent `apDecide`, `_apBusy`).
424. **CHAIN = ORGANIZATIONAL / SAP STRUCTURE, never the traveler profile**: the chain stored at submit (`cadenaIds` + `cadenaInfo`, now persisted by svSubmit / mvCompSend) or, if missing, the SAME resolver as Creación (`svChain` hospital: Dirección de Hospital fn 80001076 → Dirección Corporativa ZA02, vacancy → CH CO; corporativo: Director inmediato → Dirección Corporativa, jefe E ED → DGA; `svChainExt` externos: Autorizador primer nivel → CH CO; excedente → + CH CO step `kind:'esc'`). Capital Humano HSM never appears; Contraloría is not a travel approver; DGA participates by ROLE; nobody approves own request (resolver filters self + `apPending` excludes `r.uid === uid`); DGA own trip stays «Aprobación al enviar» (rule 13).
425. **POR AUTORIZAR = firmante EN TURNO** (`apTurn` = step `apPaso` = number of approved decisions). ONE population (`apPending`) feeds tab counter + sidebar sum (`tabCounts('aut-viaje')`, rule 418), Home «Solicitudes por autorizar» / CH CO «Casos escalados» KPI / DGA «Autorizaciones de viaje pendientes» + DGA tray travel rows, and the bell. Static demo approval rows / counts / notices are replaced, never mixed.
426. **TRAY** (Autorización › Aprobación de viaje): tabs Por autorizar · Autorizadas · Rechazadas (the last two = «resueltas por mí», reconstructed from decisions + HISTORIAL, shared list). **Por autorizar = the SAME tray component as Inicio «Solicitudes por autorizar»** (2026-10-13, one table standard): `mkTray` with `columns: 'standard'` — Folio · Colaborador (person cell) · Destino · Asunto · Detalle (fechas · monto est. / solicitada o escalamiento) · Estatus (+ Paso n de m) · Antigüedad · chevron; decision summary (personas · estimado total); search + Destino filter; pager; cards when narrow. Only difference: no exit CTA (`noCta`). Every tray header carries its filled-blue icon (Inicio included, `TRAY_ICON` by column set). Future modules that show an Inicio tray in full reuse `mkTray` instead of a new list.
427. **DETAIL TO DECIDE** (own screen, review-only, no editing of trip content): header (folio, traveler, destino/fechas, status + paso, tipo) + condition notices (otra sesión · cancelada · rechazada · aprobada · paso ya resuelto · sin permiso AU · escalada con causa · viajero externo · acompañante individual · viaje en grupo) → Presupuesto (semáforo + efecto) | Cadena de autorización (who signed, who is next, time per step, canal, escalation chip, rejection motive) → Datos del viaje (documents only in Datos del viaje › Documentos; the «Desviaciones y evidencia» card was removed, rule 445) (same `mvInfo` reading as Mis viajes, multi-destination cards, documents open) → Montos vs tarifas del perfil. No separate «Seguimiento» block (2026-10-13): the chain already shows every decision with person, date, time per step, canal and motive; the full folio history stays in Mis viajes and Bitácora. Phone: budget first, then trip/evidence/deviations. Decision bar sticky at the bottom; phone = AUTORIZAR / RECHAZAR 100 % width, stacked with 16 px gap.
428. **BUDGET = CONSUMED, NOT REDEFINED**: `apBudget` uses `PRES.disponiblePara(presData(), entidad, externo, trip start)` (monthly availability, rule 417) and `budgetTone` / `CONFIG.umbrales`. Rojo = the month's disponible does not cover the estimate → AUTORIZAR disabled with explanation (supplements = file load by Contabilidad in the Monitor, rule 419; link «Ver presupuesto en el Monitor» only when in scope); ámbar/rojo otherwise from the entity's annual utilization after committing. Entity figures (disponible, restante, %) only when the entity is inside the approver's Monitor scope (`presCaps().entidades`, rule 404); otherwise only the amount to commit + semáforo. Revalidated at the exact moment of confirming (live); outcomes suficiente · insuficiente · el saldo cambió · consulta no disponible (modal «bloqueo», nothing applied). MODO DEMO «Presupuesto al autorizar» simulates them + «Paso resuelto en otra sesión».
429. **AUTHORIZE**: confirmation always shows the money effect («Se comprometerán $X» / «al completarse la cadena» + restante in scope) and who continues → intermediate step: stays Pendiente autorización, next approver gets it (canonical tray + bell) → last step: SOL_Aprobada + append-only «Reserva» movement in the bolsa/periodo of the trip (actor = approver, Proceso) + Bitácora (Autorización + Presupuesto) + traveler notice (registrador for externos) → shared success modal → back to Por autorizar (folio gone).
430. **REJECT**: only «Detalle del motivo (opcional)» free text, max 500 (manager 2026-10-15: the quick-option catalog was REMOVED from the travel rejection modal; the anticipo rejection keeps its own catalog) → SOL_Rechazada, terminal (no resubmission invented), motive visible to the traveler (notice, Mis viajes «Qué sigue», journey stage 1 «Rechazada», chain), Bitácora with canal + días de atención.
431. **CONCURRENCY**: a step already decided (other tab/device) is blocked: the decision is not applied twice; the detail shows «Este paso ya se resolvió en otra sesión» with time and canal.
432. **INTERNAL / EXTERNAL / COMPANION**: externo never shows SAP data (no No., CECO, puesto); the companion's own folio is decided independently (origin and other companions untouched); the group origin lists each companion's folio and status.
433. **TRAVELER RESULT**: Mis viajes and Home journey read the same decisions: stage 1 «Pendiente de autorización · nivel n de m — responsable», «Aprobada · fecha · n niveles — último firmante», «Rechazada»; «Qué sigue» names the approver in turn; seed decisions appear in Seguimiento.
434. **PERMISSIONS (rule 420)**: no role change. Consultar = menu option `aut-viaje` (DIR · CHC · DGA); Autorizar = action AU of the same option resolved by `resolveAccess` (`apCaps`) — revoking AU in Usuarios y permisos leaves the tray read-only with the explanation. Chain membership is checked in `apDecide`, not only by hiding buttons.

435. **TÍTULO DE PANTALLA DE DETALLE SIN REPETIR LA TARJETA** (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-13): the page title of a detail screen names the SCREEN («Detalle de la solicitud» · «Detalle del viaje» · «Detalle de la bolsa presupuestal» · «Ficha del viajero externo»), never the record identifier that the first card already shows. The first card carries the identifier with its label («Folio 00050», never a bare number) under a contextual eyebrow (Solicitud por autorizar · Solicitud de viaje). Side-by-side cards of one row stretch to the same height (rule of symmetry).

447. **REGRESO A PENDIENTE AUTORIZACIÓN POR VIÁTICOS (client 2026-10-15, corrected the same day — supersedes the earlier «Aprobada ↔ Rechazada» version)**: default permission of Viáticos = action **CE «Cambiar estatus»** of Autorización › Aprobación de viaje (`aut-viaje` vis VIA ✓ Consultar; `actVis.CE` VIA ✓; `actVis.AU` stays DIR · CHC · DGA — Viáticos never authorizes), reflected in Usuarios y permisos (rule 420). Only at the request's AUTHORIZATION stage, one resolver `apCambioElig`: **Aprobada → Pendiente autorización** only without later movements (anticipo not cancelled/rejected, Vuelo, Anticipo or Comprobación rows, status En proceso/Finalizado) · **Rechazada → Pendiente autorización** always. In BOTH cases the request RETURNS to «Pendiente autorización» and its chain STARTS AGAIN at step 1 (`decisiones` reset; previous decisions kept in `decisionesPrev` for history, Bitácora and «resueltas por mí»; `regresoWhen` = antigüedad of step 1). Never on own request. Viáticos tray tabs «Aprobadas» / «Rechazadas» (all requests, consolidated; column «Cambio de estatus» Disponible / No disponible + reason); detail reuses the decision bar with ONE button **«Regresar a Pendiente autorización»**; modal with effect + who authorizes step 1 + **Motivo del cambio * (required)**. Effects (`apCambiar`): from Aprobada = «Liberación manual» of the folio's net reserve (evento «Regreso a Pendiente autorización por Viáticos») — the request counts again as Estimado por autorizar; from Rechazada = no budget movement (counts again as Estimado); budget is revalidated by each approver when deciding (rule 428). Notices: traveler (registrador for externos) with the motive + the first approver of the chain (tray, Home, bell). Every change appended to `r.cambios[]` (de · a · motivo · byId · when · canal) + Bitácora (Autorización + Presupuesto). CONFIRMED (client 2026-10-15): Viáticos sees ALL requests of every hospital/dirección (consolidated); any later process with confirmations (anticipo, vuelo, hospedaje, comprobación) blocks the return of an approved request. Hospedaje confirmations join the same `apCambioElig` check when the Hospedaje module (US-11) is built. **PDF (US-09)**: document this corrected rule (return to Pendiente autorización in both cases, chain restarts at step 1).

**PDF NOTE (manager 2026-10-15, documentation only — the mockup is NOT changed yet)**: in the PDF of the travel-approval story (US-09), state that requests whose **motivo de viaje = Proyecto** follow a DIFFERENT authorization chain: the authorizer is the **owner of that project's budget** (dueño del presupuesto del proyecto), not the organizational chain of rule 424 (Dirección de Hospital / Director inmediato → Dirección Corporativa). Document it as a confirmed client rule; open details to list as PENDIENTES in the same PDF: where the project owner comes from (catálogo de proyectos / SAP), whether there are further steps after the owner (Dirección Corporativa, CH CO for excedentes), what happens if the owner is the traveler (self-approval excluded → who replaces), and whether the project's own bolsa is the one consumed (request → bolsa rule, rule 403). The prototype resolver (`svChain`) does not implement this branch yet.

**PDF NOTE (manager 2026-10-15, documentation only — the mockup is NOT changed)**: in the US-09 PDF, document as a CONFIRMED client rule (authorization exceptions): when the hospital's **Dirección de Hospital is VACANT**, that authorization step passes to the **Director de Capital Humano Corporativo (CH CO)**. The prototype resolver already supports it (`svChain` vacancy branch → CH CO, list `DIRECCION_VACANTE`, empty in the demo), but there is no visible demo case; the PDF describes it as a rule (no screenshot required). Open detail to list as PENDIENTE: whether the chain then continues to Dirección Corporativa after CH CO, and the source of the vacancy flag (SAP).

### US-09 · PENDIENTES (client questions, not rules)
- Externos chain: approved US-05 resolver = Autorizador primer nivel → CH CO (2 steps); US-09 text says Director Hospital → Director Corporativo seleccionado → CH CO (3 steps). Prototype keeps the approved resolver.
- Budget figures for approvers outside the entity's Monitor scope (Dirección Corporativa / CH CO deciding a hospital request): prototype shows only amount + semáforo.
- Story link «solicitar suplemento (US-07)» superseded by rule 419 (supplements only by file load): prototype explains it and links to the Monitor.
- Whether the free-text motive is mandatory with every quick option (prototype: only «Otro motivo»); official catalog of quick options.
- Vacancy branch: resolver supports it (`DIRECCION_VACANTE`, empty in the demo); CH CO KPI «Hospitales con Dirección vacante» now reads that same list (0 · «Ninguna registrada»); a genuine vacancy example is a client data question.
- Data fixes 2026-10-13: 00052 chain = u03 → u04 by SAP structure (was a non-existent person); 00044 removed from budget PENDIENTES (request is En proceso, not por autorizar; its historical commitment has no seed row — adding one would break the seed reconciliation, left as is); static CH CO vacancy notice removed. Still static: 00041/00047 Home trips (US-02 data outside the request model).
- When the Reserva of an intermediate-step approval should exist (prototype: only at final approval, per «SOL_Aprobada — presupuesto comprometido»).

## PRESUPUESTO · ARQUITECTURA Y REGLAS (approved 2026-10-09; US-06 + US-07 in REVIEW MODE)
399. **UNA SOLA OPCIÓN DE PRESUPUESTO: PRESUPUESTO › Monitor** (approved cross-story navigation correction introduced by US-06/US-07; changes the closed US-01/US-25 baseline without reopening them). The menu option «Actualización» (`pres-act`) NO LONGER EXISTS and is never replaced by «Administración»/«Gestión» or any second budget entry. Budget administration lives inside the same Monitor workspace as permission-based tabs, sections, contextual actions, dialogs, guided flows and detail views.
400. **PERMISSION ≠ NAVIGATION.** The old Actualización access migrated to an internal capability of `pres-monitor`: actions `CO` (Consultar = navigation; DIR · CHC · DGA · VIA · CON · CTR ✓ · ADM P) + `ED` (Editar = gestionar bolsas, carga masiva, aplicar suplementos; CON ✓ · DIR P — the exact defaults Actualización had). Per-action defaults are declared with `MENU[].actVis` and read by the single resolver (`baseVis` → `roleActionState` / `resolveAccess`, rule 62). Contraloría keeps read-only audit access through Consultar. Capabilities appear in the UI only when their flow works (no dead tabs/buttons).
401. **ONE CANONICAL BUDGET MODEL** (`uspres-data.js` › `window.SMV_PRES`, rule 83/88): entidades (Hospital and Dirección Corporativa are DISTINCT entities, never merged) · bolsas by the 7 dimensions (sociedad · entidad · CECO · cuenta contable · proyecto · tipo de empleado · año) with vigencia «Desde … hasta …» and estado Activa/Inactiva · importes por estado · movimientos APPEND-ONLY with folio/evento de origen, actor/proceso, fuente and origen · Estimado por autorizar · historial por bolsa · suplementos (read-only references in M1) · alertas emitidas. Home, Monitor, Creación, cancellation and alerts consume it; no screen keeps its own budget figures (`us08-data.js › PRE_DISPONIBLE` and the HOMES budget values were removed).
402. **BUDGET CONCEPTS kept separate**: Planeado · Ampliado · Comprometido (includes En tránsito + Ejercido + reservas vigentes) · PRE disponible · Estimado (solicitudes enviadas por autorizar: validated at submit, never committed) · En tránsito · Ejercido · Liberado por cancelación. PRE disponible = Planeado − Comprometido (rule 14); whether/how Ampliado adds is a single flag (`CONFIG.ampliadoSumaAlDisponible`, demo = true per US-07 «Ampliado suma al disponible»). Home keeps the rule-261 label «Planeado − Estimado»; the Monitor shows the US-06 formula — both from `CONFIG` (tension documented, not resolved). «Consumido» stays prohibited (rule 14).
403. **ONE RESOLVER for adscripción and bolsa**: `presEntityOf(uid)` (collaborator → entity; `ADSCRIPCION` overrides) and `bolsaPara(entidad, externo)` (request → bolsa). The definitive request → bolsa rule (CECO · cuenta · proyecto · tipo de empleado) is pending and lives only there.
404. **MONITOR SCOPE BY ROLE** (`ALCANCE`, least scope wins): Dirección de Hospital = own hospital · Dirección Corporativa / CH CO = own dirección · DGA = consolidated, full detail of every hospital and dirección (client 2026-10-12) · Viáticos = consolidated read-only (pending) · Contabilidad / Contraloría = consolidated · Admin = only with individual permission, consolidated (pending) · any other role with an individual grant = its own entity. Home budget summaries never grant Monitor scope.
405. **MONITOR STRUCTURE (Milestone 1)**: scope chip · tabs Resumen | Movimientos · Resumen = KPI cards with «?» per concept (Planeado · Ampliado · Comprometido · PRE disponible + % + formula + severity · Estimado por autorizar) + desglose de lo comprometido + Entidades (consolidated only, typed Hospital / Dirección Corporativa) + Alertas activas (condición · severidad · antigüedad · estado de atención · «Ver bolsas de la entidad») + shared list «Bolsas presupuestales» (search · filters · sort · pager; cards when narrow) · Movimientos = shared list (newest first) with a movement detail panel (folio, evento, responsable, fuente; «Ver folio» only for own consultable folios) · Bolsa detail = contextual hub (dimensions, importes, alert, suplementos, historial, its movements, `presActions()` extension point — empty in M1). Suplementos and Cargas tabs are added only with their working flows.
406. **ALERTS**: computed per entity from the configurable thresholds (`CONFIG.umbrales`, demo 70 / 85 = rule 244; US-06 cites 80 / 90 — client decision pending) plus Sin disponible (PRE $0) and Sin presupuesto asignado (no active bolsa for the year). `budgetTone()` reads the same config. Home and bell notices deep-link to the SAME Monitor filtered to the entity of origin (`go('pres-monitor', { entity })`).
407. **CREACIÓN · VALIDACIÓN AL ENVIAR (US-06)**: entry precondition unchanged (entity admits = active bolsa + PRE > `admiteMinimo`); at submit the estimate (+ own requests pending approval, approved US-08 behavior) is revalidated against the live PRE. States: insuficiente · el saldo cambió durante la captura · consulta no disponible (amarillo, «Reintentar validación»). The request is never sent and stays En proceso with its folio and data (it IS the draft: no separate «Guardar borrador», rule US-08). Messages never show entity figures. MODO DEMO «Presupuesto al enviar» simulates the states.
408. **«AVISAR A MI DIRECTOR»** (Home precondition, Creación entry block and submit block): shared confirmation dialog → bell notice to the entity's Dirección (`ENTIDADES[].director`, demo recipient rule pending) with deep link to the Monitor → success modal → Bitácora event (area Presupuesto). Idempotent per folio (shows when the previous notice was sent). The collaborator is never sent to the Monitor.
409. **CANCELLATION RELEASE**: cancelling an APPROVED request appends «Liberación por cancelación» (Comprometido −, Liberado +) to the bolsa of its compromiso, by «Proceso automático», plus a Bitácora event; a pending request only stops counting as Estimado. DGA auto-approval at submit appends the corresponding Compromiso. Manual release (US-07) is a different, not-yet-built operation.
410. **TRANSICIÓN GLOBAL DE POSICIONAMIENTO EN LA MISMA PANTALLA** (permanent, all stories, 2026-10-09): whenever the Portal moves the user to another point of the CURRENT screen (lista filtrada desde una alerta o entidad, primer error al enviar/guardar, campo desde el resumen de pendientes, sección relacionada) it uses ONE shared helper `scrollToTarget(el, { offset, focus, pulse })`: smooth ease-in-out scroll of the real scroll container (280–600 ms by distance) + a brief arrival highlight on the target (azul; rojo for errors) + focus when relevant. `prefers-reduced-motion` ⇒ instant, no highlight. `jumpToError` and `svFocus` use it. A change of SCREEN (route, ficha, detalle) never animates: it opens at the top. Presentation/PDF capture scrolling stays instant.
411. **NAVEGACIÓN DEL MONITOR SIN PANELES QUE APARECEN Y DESAPARECEN** (2026-10-09): information that does not need its own screen opens in a MODAL (detalle del movimiento: folio, evento, responsable, fuente, acciones «Ver bolsa» / «Ver folio», Escape/«Cerrar», focus in and back); content that needs space has its OWN screen with the standard back button (detalle de bolsa); filters stay in place and use rule 410 to bring the filtered list into view. No inline panel is inserted above a list.
412. **AJUSTE DE TEXTO SIN CORTAR PALABRAS** (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-09): ordinary words are NEVER split mid-letter («Corporativ / o»). Text uses `overflow-wrap: break-word` (only an unbroken string longer than the whole line — correo, UUID, folio largo — may break); `overflow-wrap: anywhere` / `word-break: break-all` are prohibited (all 47 prototype uses replaced). In any row that pairs a name/label with a chip, badge or value, the row WRAPS (`flex-wrap: wrap`; text `flex: 1 1 <min>`, chip `flex: none`) so the chip drops to the next line instead of squeezing the text; `text-wrap: pretty` on short headings.
413. **MISMO TAMAÑO DE ETIQUETA POR COLUMNA** (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-09; companion of rule 145 for buttons): in every table, all status/severity chips of the SAME column share one size — the width of the widest chip (never wider than the cell) and, when one needs two lines, the same height for all; the text is CENTERED inside the chip so short labels in a wide shared chip don't look misaligned. One shared mechanism (`[data-chip]` + `syncChipW()`, run with `syncBtnW`) covers the shared list (`buildList`), the Home trays and the folio history; no screen sets chip widths of its own. Card layouts (narrow widths) keep natural chip widths.

## PRESUPUESTO · MILESTONE 2 (2026-10-12, REVIEW MODE · SYNC PENDING) — client answers 2026-10-12
414. **SUPERSEDED by rule 419 (client meeting 2026-10-13)** — no supplement lifecycle, no authorization in the Portal, no Suplementos tab, no individual request/apply. Texto original: 414. **SUPLEMENTOS**: AUTORIZAN SOLO la DGA y Contraloría (client 2026-10-12), both with consolidated Monitor, INSIDE **Presupuesto › Monitor › Suplementos** — option B: the separate tray «Autorización › Suplementos presupuestales» was REMOVED; authorization is capability `AU` of `pres-monitor` (`actVis.AU`: DGA ✓ · CTR ✓). The Suplementos tab is a CONSULTATION history of every supplement in the user's scope (all hospitals/direcciones when consolidated, own entity otherwise) — never pre-filtered by status; default order = supplements with an action pending for the user first (autorizar for DGA/Contraloría, aplicar for Contabilidad), then most recent; filter «Acción»; the tab counter counts that same population (excludes own requests); row action «Revisar» only where an action is pending, «Ver detalle» otherwise; after deciding, the supplement stays listed with its new status; bell notices deep-link to that tab. Every role only sees/does what its capabilities allow; SOLICITA Dirección (DIR, own scope) y Contabilidad; APLICA Contabilidad (capability Editar). Lifecycle Pendiente de autorización → Autorizado (documento de soporte OBLIGATORIO) | Rechazado (motivo) → Aplicado (Ampliado, once) · Cancelado (solicitante, antes de autorizarse, motivo). A sent supplement is never edited. Request is pre-filled with the déficit = folios bloqueados + Estimado por autorizar − PRE disponible (`presDeficit`; folios from `BLOQUEOS` + session «Avisar a mi Director»). Applying unblocks those folios and notifies each requester (bell → Creación) and the solicitante; segregation: the authorizer cannot authorize own request.
415. **MÍNIMO DE REDUCCIÓN = COMPROMETIDO** (client): Comprometido already includes ejercido, en tránsito, reservas and every applied extra; never add Ejercido again. Message states the minimum and its composition. Comprometido is also the maximum anticipo the collaborator may request; at Cierre the unused committed is released (movement type «Liberación al cierre» defined in the model; the Cierre flow is not built).
416. **PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED by rule 419**: tabs are now Resumen · Movimientos · Cargas (no Suplementos); no «Solicitar suplemento», no «Editar bolsa / Editar montos» (amounts change ONLY through Cargar archivo); Liberación manual · Cerrar vigencia · exports remain. Texto original: 416. **ADMINISTRACIÓN DENTRO DEL MONITOR**: tabs by capability Resumen · Movimientos (all) · Suplementos (DIR · DGA · CON · CTR) · Cargas (CON · CTR read-only). Actions only where their flow works: Nueva bolsa (CON) · Exportar monitor / movimientos / suplementos (CON · CTR, CSV with active filters) · bolsa detail: Solicitar suplemento · Editar bolsa (importe + vigencia; dimensions read-only) · Liberación manual (folio + importe ≤ comprometido del folio y reservas + motivo) · Cerrar vigencia (motivo; logical, nothing deleted). Every mutation = focused dialog → confirmation before → after → movement (append-only) + bolsa history + Bitácora (area Presupuesto).
417. **PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED by rule 419**: each month cell is now a signed MOVEMENT classified automatically (no «actualiza la bolsa mes por mes» / «Ajuste de planeado» / «Editar montos»); file format, bolsa key, División→entidad, separate Nombre de la Div. · Div. · Denominación columns, months with year and MONTHLY AVAILABILITY stay in force. Texto original: 417. **CARGA MASIVA = ÚNICA FORMA DE CARGAR PRESUPUESTO** (client 2026-10-12, supersedes the individual «Nueva bolsa»): no individual bolsa creation in the Portal and no catalogs for load fields — they always come from the file. File format = the client's budget workbook: Soc. · Nombre de la Div. (nombre del hospital) · Div. (clave del hospital) · Denominación (código del hospital) · Cta.mayor (cuenta de gasto) · Texto explicativo (descripción) · Ce.coste (CECO) · Posición Ptto (posición presupuestal) · CeBe (centro de beneficio) + Enero…Diciembre (monto variable por mes; «-»/vacío = 0), CSV, ejercicio chosen in the Cargas bar. Bolsa key = Soc + Div + Ce.coste + Cta.mayor + Posición Ptto + CeBe + ejercicio; an existing key = ACTUALIZA the bolsa month by month (only changed months, «Ajuste de planeado» per month; total never below Comprometido); new key = new bolsa with ALL 12 months of the ejercicio (months without amount in the file exist at $0 and can be edited later; one «Carga» movement per month with amount). Month labels always carry the year («Enero 2026»). «Tipo de empleado» and «Proyecto» are no longer bolsa dimensions. División → entidad: hospital via `DIVISIONES` or «Cat. Hospital» name; corporate division 11NM split by CeBe prefix (`CORP_CEBE`, demo). Per-row preview validation, error report, explicit partial apply, batch history CAR-000n. Every movement carries its PERIODO (mes de vigencia) and the bolsa's load fields (shown in the movement modal and the Movimientos list). Bolsa table shows Vigencia (desde – hasta, meses con presupuesto); bolsa detail shows «Presupuesto planeado por mes» (current month highlighted); «Editar montos» = the 12 monthly amounts only. MONTHLY AVAILABILITY (client 2026-10-12): a request falls in the bolsa of its entity and in the MONTH of its trip start (`disponiblePara`); per bolsa and month Disponible = Planeado del mes (+ Ampliado applied that month) − Comprometido del mes (`mensual`; seed comprometido assigned chronologically from January — demo). Home «admite nuevas solicitudes» = current-month disponible of the entity (`preMes`); Creación validates the trip month; DGA auto-commit and cancellation release carry that periodo. Monitor: KPI «Disponible · <mes>», table column «Disponible <mes>», detail grid per month (planeado · comprometido · disponible). Alerts / % utilizado stay annual. **CONTROL MENSUAL SIN ARRASTRE (client 2026-10-13)**: what is committed in a month never exceeds that month's budget (planeado + suplementos − bloqueos); unused budget of closed months does NOT carry over (`mensual`: per-month pre, `preUsable` = current month + following months = «PRE disponible · resto del año», `noAcum` shown as «sin usar en meses cerrados no se acumula»). Monthly comprometido comes from the rows by periodo (`compPorMes`, same counting as the Movimientos de bolsa header); seed rows are generated month by month within each month's cap. Every KPI card states ANUAL or MENSUAL (Home + Monitor); in Monitor › Resumen the cards are TWO ROWS with the SAME six concepts — «Anual · ejercicio aaaa» (Planeado · Ampliado · Bloqueado · Comprometido · PRE disponible resto del año · Estimado por autorizar) above «Mensual · <mes aaaa>» (Planeado · Ampliado · Bloqueado · Comprometido · Disponible · Estimado por autorizar del mes, from `entidad.mesAct`; monthly cards = tenue blue #F2FAFD / #B3E3F5); all 12 cards share one size (grid rows 1fr = symmetry rule) and the «Desglose de lo comprometido» block was REMOVED for every role (manager 2026-10-14): Disponible del mes · <mes aaaa> (decides admission) · Planeado anual · Comprometido anual · PRE disponible · resto del año. Nombre de la Div. · Div. · Denominación are shown as SEPARATE columns/fields (bolsa table, movimientos, carga preview, movement modal, detail).
418. **CONTADORES DE PESTAÑA Y SUMA EN EL MENÚ** (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-12): a tab that holds pending work for the user shows a counter badge (red #F25757, 20 px, square; contador = población, rule 84 — only work the user can act on, own requests excluded). The sidebar option of that module shows the SUM of its tab counters (overrides the static demo count). ONE source `tabCounts(route, user)` feeds tabs and menu; `tabBadge()` builds the badge. Applied: Usuarios y permisos › Usuarios (the Monitor › Suplementos counter was removed with rule 419) (active users without a vigente role or with Portal configuration pending, Admin). Tabs without an actionable population (Resumen, Movimientos, Cargas, Roles y permisos, Perfil del viajero tabs) show no counter. Future tabbed modules register their populations in `tabCounts`.

**PDF NOTE (manager 2026-10-14, documentation only — the mockup is NOT changed)**: in the US-06/US-07 PDF, «Exportar monitor» must be specified as an export of the MOVEMENT DETAIL ONLY (no KPI summary / bolsa totals), with ALL the movement information: tipo · periodo · fecha · importe con signo · bolsa and its load fields (Soc. · Nombre de la Div. · Div. · Denominación · Cta.mayor · Texto explicativo · Ce.coste · Posición Ptto · CeBe · ejercicio) · folio · viajero · evento · responsable/registró · fuente · carga/archivo · documento de autorización — Movimientos presupuestales (only with MP) and Movimientos de bolsa, respecting the user's scope and active filters.

## PRESUPUESTO · CLIENT MEETING CORRECTIONS (2026-10-13, REVIEW MODE · SYNC PENDING) — supersede 414 and parts of 416/417
419. **DOS CONCEPTOS DE MOVIMIENTO, NUNCA MEZCLADOS** (`uspres-data.js › MOV_PRES / MOV_BOLSA`): **MOVIMIENTOS PRESUPUESTALES** = Carga inicial · Suplemento · Bloqueo (modifican el presupuesto de la bolsa) · Cierre de vigencia (afecta la bolsa de forma global, no es gasto; sin importe ni documento) · **MOVIMIENTOS DE BOLSA** = Anticipos y Comprobaciones + changes made in the Portal (Liberación manual · Cierre de vigencia) labeled «Cambio en el Portal». ALL budget changes enter through **CARGAR ARCHIVO** (Cargas tab, CON); each signed month cell of a row is classified by bolsa + MONTH: first positive amount of the month = **Carga inicial** (no document) · later positive in the same month = **Suplemento** (applied on registration; Ampliado +) · negative = **Bloqueo** (Bloqueado +, reduces disponible; never deletion, closing, cancellation or manual release; cannot exceed the month's disponible). Suplemento and Bloqueo require the **DOCUMENTO DE AUTORIZACIÓN**, one per affected BOLSA (multiplicity per movement not defined → minimum = per bolsa), uploaded in the preview (shared 5 MB validator, clickable dummy preview); movements without their document are not applied (explicit partial apply with report). No supplement statuses, no authorization tray, no «Aplicar», no status column. Formula: PRE disponible = Planeado + Suplementos − Bloqueos − Comprometido (`CONFIG.formula`; monthly the same per month). Monitor tabs: Resumen · Movimientos · Cargas. **Movimientos tab** = 1 Movimientos presupuestales → 2 Movimientos de bolsa, global to the user's scope; **bolsa detail** = the SAME two components (`presList` / `bolList`) scoped to one bolsa. **No «Historial de cambios»** (client 2026-10-13): every change of a bolsa is a row of one of the two tables — loads in Movimientos presupuestales (origin = carga) and changes made in the Portal (Liberación manual · Cierre de vigencia) in Movimientos de bolsa, identified by their own movement type (no «Origen» column in either table — client 2026-10-13: amounts change only through the load file, so origin adds nothing). Movement modal adapts by type (presupuestal: tipo, importe con signo y efecto, periodo, bolsa, carga/archivo, documento «Ver documento», registró · de bolsa: folio, viajero, evento, responsable, «Ver folio» if own). Compromisos (incl. the flight's direct consumption) and liberaciones stay in the model/amounts but are not rows of either table. **ETIQUETAS DE MOVIMIENTOS DE BOLSA (manager 2026-10-14)**: the internal type «Reserva» is DISPLAYED as **«Solicitud de viaje»** (= monto comprometido del viaje: estimado total por tarifas máximas del perfil) in the table, filter, chip and modal (`TL()` display map; data type unchanged). Lifecycle shown per row: Solicitud de viaje (Comprometido vigente) → Anticipo (se descuenta del comprometido del viaje → **En tránsito · por comprobar**) → Comprobación (Ejercido; the anticipo row reads «Comprobado · pasó a Comprobaciones»). Anticipo row status (manager 2026-10-14): **En autorización** (pendAut) → **En tránsito · por depositar** (autorizado / contabilizado / por transferir; devuelto = «por depositar de nuevo») → **Depositado · por comprobar** → **Comprobado · pasó a Comprobaciones**. The first two are FOLLOW-UP rows (`antSeg`, origen «Seguimiento», read-only, NO budget effect, excluded from reconciliation/cards); the accounting row with amount is still born at the confirmed deposit (US-15). **VUELO (client 2026-10-15)** = new Movimientos de bolsa type: it comes from the travel request, never goes through the anticipo and is the only concept that takes budget DIRECTLY: row «Vuelo» **En tránsito · por confirmar el boleto** → **Confirmado · pasó a Comprobaciones** (counts as Ejercido); its amount is deducted from the vigente comprometido of ITS folio (the «Solicitud de viaje» row reads «Comprometido vigente · sin el vuelo»). Same counting in `compPorMes` (data) and `reconOf` (Monitor). Seed: synthetic Vuelo rows + their folio's Reserva, reconciling every bolsa. PENDING: the live flight purchase/confirmation flow (Vuelos, US-11/US-13) is not built — no session Vuelo rows yet. Reconciliation chips in order: Solicitudes de viaje vigentes + Anticipos depositados por comprobar + Vuelos por confirmar + Comprobaciones (incl. vuelos confirmados) = Comprometido. This supersedes «Compromisos (incl. the flight's direct consumption) … are not rows». PENDING: the manager says the trip amount is comprometido «antes y después de aprobarse»; today a submitted request is only «Estimado por autorizar» (rule 402/US-06, never committed) and the row appears at final approval — not changed until confirmed.
**CONCILIACIÓN** (2026-10-13): every bolsa figure must add up from its rows — Ejercido = Σ Comprobaciones · En tránsito = Σ Anticipos por comprobar · Reservas vigentes = Σ «Reserva» rows (compromiso del folio aprobado, incl. vuelo) still vigentes (folio sin anticipo/comprobación, neto de liberaciones); Movimientos de bolsa now = Reserva · Anticipo · Comprobación · Liberación por cancelación · Liberación manual; the reconciliation (Comprobaciones + Anticipos por comprobar + Reservas vigentes = Comprometido) is a compact chip row in the HEADER of Movimientos de bolsa (bolsa detail and Movimientos tab, scoped), no separate block. Seed rows are synthetic and generated so each seed bolsa reconciles exactly.
420. **CONSISTENCIA DE PERMISOS (permanent, global, 2026-10-13)**: every change to what a role can see/do in a module is made in the canonical permission config + single resolver (`MENU[].actVis` + `us26-data.js › ITEM_ACTIONS/ACTIONS` → `resolveAccess`) so feature behavior, Usuarios y permisos (role editor, general matrix, per-user matrix) and the data-visibility layer always agree. Checklist for EVERY permission change: 1 feature behavior · 2 canonical config/resolver · 3 Usuarios y permisos shows it · 4 matrix read-only/write representation · 5 regression on one affected and one unaffected role. Never restrict only with CSS or only inside a feature screen.
421. **VIÁTICOS SIN MOVIMIENTOS PRESUPUESTALES** (client 2026-10-13, first application of rule 420): new action **MP «Ver movimientos presupuestales»** of Presupuesto › Monitor (DIR · CHC · DGA · CON · CTR ✓ · ADM P · **VIA none**). Without MP the Carga inicial / Suplemento / Bloqueo rows are filtered out of the data (Movimientos tab, bolsa detail, movement modal lookup, exports), the Cargas tab is hidden; Viáticos keeps Resumen and Movimientos de bolsa. The old action AU (autorizar suplementos) was removed from Monitor.
422. **FILTRO DE PERIODO = CALENDARIO MENSUAL (permanent, global UI rule, 2026-10-13)**: a table filtered by period never lists every month of every year in a dropdown. It uses the shared month-range picker (`periodPicker`, `filters[].type = 'periodo'`, row field `periodoK` = aaaa + mm): button «Periodo · <rango>» with calendar icon → popover with year navigation (only years around the data) and a 3×4 month grid; first click = desde, second = hasta (same month twice = one month); months without data are dimmed but selectable; «Todos» clears; Escape/«Cerrar» closes. Applied to Movimientos presupuestales, Movimientos de bolsa (tab + bolsa detail) and the Cargas preview. Any future table with a period dimension uses it.

## USER STORY PDF STANDARD (permanent, 2026-10-08 — first applied to US-03)
395. Every finalized User Story PDF is BOTH the client visual approval record AND the development functional specification. It must include: (1) real implementation screenshots; (2) functional behavior implemented by the mockup; (3) business rules applied by the story; (4) roles/permissions; (5) validations; (6) states/actions/transitions; (7) data ownership/source (Portal · SAP · catalog · calculated · snapshot); (8) the GLOBAL UI/UX rules the module materially relies on (not the whole rulebook); (9) responsive behavior (desktop · medium/tablet · phone); (10) implementation/development notes (entities, canonical sources, shared helpers, state ownership); (11) acceptance-criteria mapping; (12) open assumptions / pending decisions in a SEPARATE section, never mixed with approved rules. Structure: Portada · Objetivo y alcance · Roles y permisos · Acceso y navegación · Flujo funcional · Reglas funcionales · Validaciones · Estados/transiciones · Datos y fuente · UI/componentes · Escenarios (normal, alterno, error/bloqueo, solo lectura, variante por rol/perfil) · Responsivo · Accesibilidad/interacción · Modelo funcional/implementación · Reglas globales aplicadas · Supuestos/pendientes · Criterios de aceptación · Historial/versión.
396. CODE → rendered implementation → captures → PDF remains the source-of-truth chain. The PDF never describes behavior that does not exist in the synchronized implementation unless it is explicitly marked pending/future; superseded rules never appear as active behavior (only the final result, optionally in the change history). Naming stays «Propuesta visual (US-0n - <título canónico vigente>)».
397. CAPTURE HARNESS NOTE: the capture renderer lays out `<button>`/`<select>` differently from the live page; `_capture.dc.html` freezes them with their live geometry before capturing (capture-only). Verify a suspicious capture against the live DOM before "fixing" product code; content edits to the DC may need a fresh load of the harness (open another file, then the harness) to be picked up.

## USER STORY ORDER + PDF NAMING (permanent, 2026-09-15)
- User Stories may be implemented OUT OF NUMERICAL ORDER; never assume lower-numbered stories are done or must precede. Work only the US explicitly provided, and never infer its scope from its number.
- Every feature/US PDF generated at FINALIZATION is titled and named with the prefix «Propuesta visual (FEATURE)» — e.g. `Propuesta visual (US-26 - Nombre de la historia).pdf`. No PDF during REVIEW MODE unless explicitly requested.

## NEXT USER STORY WORKFLOW (permanent)
1 read CLAUDE.md → 2 read PROJECT_WORKFLOW.md → 3 recover the current cumulative source (`handoff/Portal-Viaticos-Prototype/`) + prototype (`US-01 Layout Portal.dc.html`, `us01-data.js`) → 4 read the new User Story → 5 reconcile with project documentation → 6 EXTEND the existing prototype (never recreate) → 7 reuse the approved US-01 shell/layout/components/tokens → 8 work in REVIEW MODE during client iteration → 9 no final PDF/source/ZIP until explicitly requested → 10 before moving to another US, remind the manager if SYNC PENDING exists.

## WORKFLOW PHASES (permanent, 2026-09-14) — replaces «every correction updates source + PDF + ZIP»
- **REVIEW MODE (default while a US is under client review):** update ONLY the interactive prototype (`US-01 Layout Portal.dc.html`, `us01-data.js`, `us01-photo.js`, minimal config) so the client can validate quickly. Do NOT regenerate PDF, modular source, standalone HTML, ZIP, manifests or the documentation set. Record permanent business/UX rules concisely here; log pending propagation in `FEATURE_SYNC_PENDING.md` (state SYNC PENDING). The prototype is the temporary source of truth; source/PDF lagging is intentional.
- **FINALIZATION (only on explicit request: «Finaliza la historia», «Cierra US-0n», «Genera los entregables finales», «Actualiza código y PDF», «Prepara el código para GitHub»):** take the current cumulative source + approved prototype changes → sync modular source, JSON mocks, assets, docs (HOME/MENU/TABLE matrices, README, IMPLEMENTATION_MAP, ASSET_MANIFEST), PDF (captures from the implementation), standalone, ZIP, persistent context, regression check. Never rebuild. Then mark SYNC COMPLETE and clear `FEATURE_SYNC_PENDING.md`.
- **BEFORE A NEW US:** if the previous US is SYNC PENDING, do not start; ask: «The previous User Story has approved prototype changes not yet synchronized to the final source/PDF/GitHub package. Finalize/sync it before starting the next User Story?» and wait. Never finalize automatically.
- Terminology: REVIEW MODE · SYNC PENDING · FINALIZATION · SYNC COMPLETE.

## Working mode
- One User Story at a time; wait for explicit US before designing. No production code deliverables — rendered visual proposals only (HTML is internal rendering, not implementation).
- Source priority: current US > approved flow (Portal_Viaticos_Starmedica_Flujo_Unico_Roles_v02) > Roles PPT > Alcance V2 > Starmedica Style Guidelines > client mockup (reference only).
- Extracted document text lives in notes/ (alcance.txt, roles.txt, flujo.txt, mockup.txt).
- Permanent rule: CODE → rendered implementation → captures → PDF applies at FINALIZATION. PDFs (client + dev audience) never show demo/debug controls or editor UI; captures at 1440×900 / 1280×800 / 390×844 via the package's presentation-mode embeds, never the narrow editor preview.

## Client-approved rules (US-01 review 2026-09-11) — PERMANENT, override earlier visual decisions
1. NO ROUNDED CORNERS: every rectangular UI surface (cards, containers, panels, buttons, inputs, dropdowns, menus, message bars, notices, account menu, dialogs, nav items, status chips, counters, tables, filters, tooltips) uses `--radius-default: 0px`.
2. Circular semantic elements (avatar, notification dot, journey-step marker, progress indicator, radio) stay circular via `--radius-circle: 50%`. They are not "rounded rectangles". Never introduce pill buttons/chips as a workaround; keep no other radius tokens.
3. User identity shows the CLIENT-SUPPLIED demo photograph (`public/assets/users/demo-profile-user.jpg`, users.json › photo; DC: `assets/users/`), initials only as fallback. Never real employees, private data, or external URLs — the repo may be public.
4. Traveler journey status = ONE shared component (`src/shared/journey-progress.js`; DC: the journey block in the trip card) wherever a traveler-capable user sees their OWN trip, regardless of functional role. Markers 40 px desktop / 36 px mobile, vertical on mobile.
5. Canonical main stages, always exactly four: Aprobación de viaje · Anticipo · Comprobación · Cierre. Hospedaje/Vuelo are subprocess statuses, never stages.
6. HOME IS ROLE- AND SCOPE-AWARE. `home.json › summary.scope`: `personal` (own travel figures) · `operational` (own work queue) · `organizational` (unit budget). Regular collaborators see personal travel information, never Dirección budget totals. Budget totals only for roles with documented responsibility + Presupuesto › Monitor: Dirección de Hospital (own hospital), Dirección Corporativa (own dirección), Contraloría (consolidated). Personal and organizational values never share a field.
7. Sidebar collapse/expand is a SHELL UTILITY, not a navigation item: FIXED footer of the sidebar OUTSIDE the scrollable list (always visible, no scrolling needed), divider, secondary weight (12 px Medium, 85 % opacity, full on hover/focus), icon + title/aria-label when collapsed, absent on mobile. Never adjacent to Inicio.
7b. Sidebar = [navigation list: the ONLY part that scrolls, vertically] + [utility footer]. Fixed width in both states; overflow-x hidden — the collapsed rail must NEVER show horizontal scroll. Collapsed mode REMOVES labels/group titles from layout (not clipped, not off-screen); items are named by title + aria-label; counters are compact 16 px badges inside the item box (top-right) and never widen the 72 px rail.
7c. ACCOUNT PANEL = fields ONLY: Número de colaborador · Dirección · Puesto · Perfil (traveler profile, readable label e.g. «Mando medio» — distinct from the functional role; NEVER «Nivel n» or internal codes) + separator + «Cerrar sesión». It NEVER repeats photo/name/role: the HEADER is the primary identity area. Data: users.json › employeeNumber / direccion / puesto / perfil.label.
7k. USER IDENTITY REDUNDANCY (Portal-wide): never repeat information already visible in the triggering component; panels opened from the header show complementary data + actions only.
7d. Colaborador Home KPIs are PERSONAL ONLY: Comprometido · En tránsito · Gasto acumulado · Uso de presupuesto asignado (% of the collaborator's OWN assigned travel budget; home.json › summary.personalBudget, mock display value). NEVER Dirección totals nor Dirección consumption % (previous «Consumo de la Dirección» was rejected).
7j. HOME SCOPE: every KPI has an explicit scope; traveler capability NEVER escalates data access (adds own trip only); organizational budget indicators need documented responsibility (Presupuesto › Monitor ✓) or permission P; operational roles get work-queue indicators, not inherited budget cards. Canonical reference: `handoff/…/docs/HOME_VISIBILITY_MATRIX.md` — update it whenever a US changes Home visibility; least scope wins.
7e. COPY: contextual/help text is neutral (no «tu/tus»: «Aquí se muestra…»); personal wording only in user actions («Registra tus gastos», «Ver mis viajes»).
7f. DATES state their meaning: notices render «Notificado: <timestamp>» (notifications.json › notifiedAt / us01-data when); business dates (vence, viaje, depósito) live inside text/Detalle. Never a bare date.
7g. Home main journey card = the MOST RECENT trip/request only (others in Mis viajes); shown even at stage 1 (Aprobación de viaje current).
7h. Tray column sets (home.json › trays[].columns): personal = Folio · Destino · Asunto · Estatus · Antigüedad · externos (CH HSM) = Folio · Viajero · Destino · Asunto · Detalle (ALWAYS with dates) · Estatus · Antigüedad · operational (default). Empty tray → short text; for CH HSM without external pendings the own traveler card predominates.
7i. Canonical demo photo = client-supplied `public/assets/users/demo-profile-user.jpg` (DC: `assets/users/`), u01 only; never substitute.
8. Every client correction goes to the prototype first (REVIEW MODE); source → PDF → docs → ZIP propagate at FINALIZATION only (see WORKFLOW PHASES). Extend, never rebuild.
9. EVERY future client-validation document is DIFFED against the current implementation before coding: classify each observation as already satisfied / requires change / open, implement only changes, never re-implement satisfied items, never invent behavior for open questions (log them in docs/CLIENT_OPEN_ITEMS_*.md).

## Client-approved rules (Validación Mockup V2, 2026-09-14) — PERMANENT
10. ACCOUNT PANEL fields: Número de colaborador · Dirección · Hospital (ONLY hospital-based users: users.json › hospital; corporate users have no Hospital field, never invent one) · Puesto · Perfil + «Cerrar sesión».
11. PROFILE VOCABULARY (user-facing perfil.label): Director · Ejecutivo · Mando medio · Operativo; DGA = unique profile «DGA». Never «Nivel 1/2/3» anywhere (labels, table sub-lines, mock data).
12. FOLIO TERMINOLOGY: in prose/messages/cards the request number is introduced as «folio 000nn» («Nueva solicitud folio 00045»). A table cell under a FOLIO header holds the number only; never «Folio folio».
13. JOURNEY «NO APLICA»: stages stay exactly 1 Aprobación de viaje · 2 Anticipo · 3 Comprobación · 4 Cierre, never renumbered; a non-applicable stage uses state `na` (gray-filled marker #D9D8D6, number kept, note «No aplica»). ANTICIPO RULE (client, final «Solo a Director grupo E ED»): Anticipo = No aplica ONLY for perfil **Director** AND personnelGroup **E or ED** (users.json › personnelGroup; single rule `anticipoApplies`). DGA is a distinct profile and does NOT inherit it (no independent approved DGA rule exists) — never «all Directors», never «DGA always». DGA own travel: Aprobación de viaje starts approved at capture (no superior approver).
14. ORGANIZATIONAL BUDGET block (roles with Presupuesto › Monitor and documented responsibility): Planeado · Ejercido · Comprometido · PRE disponible. **Ejercido** is the canonical user-facing term (never «Consumido»); **PRE disponible = Planeado − Comprometido** (definitive; Ejercido does not change it). CH CO sees its OWN dirección budget + escalated cases; a Home may hold several KPI blocks (home.json › summaries[]).
15. STANDARD REQUEST LIST (Dirección de Hospital, Dirección Corporativa, CH CO): Folio · Colaborador / Viajero (sub-line: Hospital o Corporativo · área · perfil) · Destino · Asunto · Detalle (ALWAYS with dates) · Estatus · Antigüedad (columns `standard`).
16. ORGANIZATIONAL ORIGIN: consolidated operational queues (Hotelería HSM, Hotelería Corporativo, Viáticos, Contabilidad/Tesorería, Contraloría, DGA) show a «Hospital / Corporativo» column (`operationalOrg`, row.org). Personal traveler views (Mis viajes) never gain organizational columns.
17. CH CO is traveler-capable in the demo (own trip card, Planeación de viaje incl. Solicitudes/Mis viajes and Gastos de representación by profile); Autorización › Aprobación de viaje stays ✓. CH CO authorizes escalated cases only. Help copy: «Aquí llegan solo los casos escalados de: hospitales con Dirección vacante, excedentes de política o presupuesto, autorizaciones específicas.»
18. DGA Home queue label: «Autorizaciones de viaje pendientes» (UI label only).
19. HOTELERÍA HSM Home: concise statistics Satisfacción de huésped · Satisfacción de la estancia (promedio) · Índice de incidencias (quejas / estancias) · Nivel de rechazos = percentage counter ALWAYS shown in red (kpi.valueTone); NO threshold logic exists or is required.
20. ICONS: same function = same icon across roles; every Comprobación / Comprobaciones item uses `fileCheck` (document + check). Update only the central icon map (us01-data.js ICONS / src/config/icons.js + navigation.json).
21. NAMING: «Vuelos» (plural) for module/menu labels (Planeación de viaje › Vuelos, Reservaciones › Hotel y vuelos); prose about «un vuelo» unchanged. Viáticos: «Pendiente de reembolso $monto» replaces «Falta ticket»; never «$$».
22. ADMIN bitácora columns (final): Fecha y hora · Folio («—» when none, never invented) · Usuario · Área · Perfil (approved vocabulary) · Acción · Resultado (`bitacora` column set).

## Client clarifications (Validación V2 FINAL, 2026-09-14) — PERMANENT, close the V2 round
23. DIRECCIÓN DE HOSPITAL Home adds «Hospedados actualmente · <hospital>» (columns `hosted`: Folio · Colaborador / Viajero · Hospital / Corporativo (origen) · Entrada · Salida · Estatus · Habitación): guests of the OWN hospital only.
24. DIRECCIÓN CORPORATIVA and CH CO Homes add «Colaboradores en viaje · <dirección>» (columns `traveling`: Folio · Colaborador / Viajero · Hospital / Corporativo · Destino · Fechas · Estatus · Regreso): collaborators of the OWN organizational scope only («los suyos, no todos»).
25. VIÁTICOS › Perfil del viajero = L (Lectura): read-only consultation of any traveler's profile; no edit actions.
26. TABLE SEMANTICS: within one table every column means the SAME thing for every row; Detalle has ONE documented meaning per table (trays[].detalleMeaning) — never approval history in one row and dates in another. Hospital / Corporativo is a dedicated column where operationally relevant, never in personal views. Canonical reference: `handoff/…/docs/TABLE_LIST_STANDARD.md`.
27. FILTERS + SORTING + PAGINATION are REQUIRED on every scalable collection (Mis viajes, solicitudes, autorizaciones, externos, colas operativas, hospedados, colaboradores en viaje, bitácora, catálogos, reportes) and must FUNCTION in the prototype against the mock data: search + contextual filters (status / org / perfil declared per tray) · sortable headers with direction indicator · pager «Página x de y» + prev/next. Not for KPI cards, journey, help text. One shared implementation (`src/shared/data-list.js`; DC: tray pipeline in renderVals). Future US apply this standard.
28. «NIVEL» is PROHIBITED for personnel/traveler classification in every user-facing text and mock dataset (users, home, notifications, PDF, docs) → always «Perfil» + approved vocabulary. Metric names such as «Nivel de rechazos» are allowed. Internal rules use `personnelGroup` (E / ED / EJ / MM / OP), never exposed as «Nivel».
29. STANDALONE build (`publish/US-01 Portal Viaticos.html`) and the published artifact show the demo photo offline: `us01-photo.js` (data URL, loaded from the DC helmet; prototype/bundle only) — avatar renders it as an `<img>`; the modular source keeps the normal file asset.
30. Every future feature EXTENDS the cumulative source and applies rules 10–29; never rebuild. Full propagation happens at FINALIZATION.

## US-26 (backlog actual US-25) · Usuarios y permisos / Bitácora — APPROVED · CLOSED · FULLY SYNCED 2026-10-09 (recorded 2026-09-15)
31. TWO DIMENSIONS, NEVER MERGED: rol funcional + ámbito vs capacidad de viajero. Both are administered in the Portal (see rule 55); no role grants own travel, and traveler capability is never an individual menu permission.
32. PERMISOS EFECTIVOS = matriz base del rol (US-01) + overrides vigentes del usuario; en conflicto la REVOCACIÓN gana. Un override nunca reescribe la matriz base; «Restaurar default» revoca overrides y conserva su historial. La UI distingue siempre DEFAULT/heredado (azul) de AJUSTE MANUAL (ámbar) y REVOCADO (rojo), con marca (✓/✕/—) + etiqueta, nunca solo color.
33. Todo editor de asignaciones/permisos muestra el efecto en LENGUAJE LLANO antes de guardar («Con estos roles, María podrá…») y la alerta de SEGREGACIÓN DE FUNCIONES explica el conflicto concreto.
34. Un usuario activo exige al menos un rol; los roles con ámbito obligatorio no se guardan sin ámbito; la baja/suspensión con folios en gestión exige reasignación previa.
35. BITÁCORA: solo lectura absoluta para todos (incluido Admin/Seguridad TI); rango de fechas OBLIGATORIO; aviso antes de ejecutar rangos amplios; cada evento muestra valor anterior → nuevo; vistas predefinidas (excepciones, tarifas, autorizaciones lentas, accesos a datos sensibles, tiempos por etapa); exportación con los filtros como constancia; consulta fuera de ámbito = acceso denegado explicado. Estados: usuario Activo/Suspendido/Baja · permiso Vigente/Revocado.
36. Prototype files: US-26 mock data lives in `us26-data.js` (`window.SMV_US26`), loaded from the DC helmet like `us01-data.js`. Pending propagation: `FEATURE_SYNC_PENDING.md`.

## ROLES, PERMISOS Y VIGENCIAS (permanent, 2026-09-15) — resolved with the client, override earlier assumptions
37. **US-01 IS THE SOURCE OF TRUTH** for the existing role catalog, role distinctions, default menu/permission visibility, read-only (L) vs actionable options, traveler rules and organizational scope. A later US may EXTEND it only when it explicitly introduces a capability US-01 does not define; it must never silently rewrite, rename, merge or collapse it. Generic wording in a later story never beats the more specific approved US-01 definition; a genuine contradiction is REPORTED, not resolved. US-26 consumes the matrix live (`window.SMV_US01.MENU` → defaults; `us26-data.js` only maps option→section and option→actions).
38. Dirección de Hospital and Dirección Corporativa are SEPARATE roles (independent assignment, scope, validity and effective access) even when a story writes «Dirección de Hospital/Corporativa»: that is conceptual grouping. The matrix shows 12 role rows (no Nómina); both read the approved «DIR» defaults.
39. Contraloría keeps READ-ONLY (L) audit access with its approved scope; «Reportes de control» is only a contextual entry point, never administrative capability. The same audit view is reused with restricted scope — no duplicated logic.
40. **NO OPEN-ENDED MANUAL ASSIGNMENTS.** Every manual role assignment AND every manual permission override requires fecha inicio + fecha fin, motivo and actor/audit metadata. No «Sin vencimiento», «Indefinido» or blank end date. Validation: fin > inicio, no creating an already-expired range as active, conflicting overlaps detected (incompatible roles only clash when their validities overlap). Statuses derive from dates + revocation: Programado · Vigente · Vencido · Revocado.
41. EXPIRATION: on expiry the override/assignment stops applying and the effective permission is resolved again from the approved baseline + other valid rules. The default matrix is never mutated. «Restaurar default» revokes the user's overrides (history kept) and returns to the baseline.
42. Effective permissions = US-01 baseline defaults (resolved with traveler capability and profile flags) + US-26 scoped manual changes; revocation wins. Never a second competing default model. Admin/Seguridad TI administers who has a role, its scope and validity, plus approved exceptions — it cannot invent capabilities outside the approved catalog.
43. DATE FIELDS — REUSABLE PORTAL PATTERN: one shared date input (text dd/mm/aaaa + visible calendar button + month calendar with today/selected/disabled days, range-aware min/max, reserved validation line, rectangular surfaces, Roboto). Display format is ALWAYS dd/mm/aaaa; mock values may stay machine-readable. Range pairs are labelled Fecha inicio / Fecha fin (or Desde / Hasta for filters). Future stories reuse this pattern — no second calendar component.
44. PERSON SEARCH — REUSABLE PATTERN: wherever a user has Número de colaborador, search matches name AND employee number, normalized (trim, case, accents, digits); only meaningful identification fields are searched. Empty result = explicit message + «Limpiar filtros». Active filters show a count + clear action.
45. Segregation of duties: the concept applies, but the exact incompatible role pairs must come from approved requirements. The current 4 pairs are DEMO data (`us26-data.js › CONFLICTS`, flag `CONFLICTS_DEMO`), labelled as such in the UI and pending client confirmation.
46. Bitácora «Origen» (Web / APP) is an APPROVED US-26 extension of the US-01 baseline: columns = Fecha y hora · Origen · Folio · Usuario · Área · Perfil · Acción · Resultado (rule 22 is updated by this extension at FINALIZATION). The >90-day range notice is a UX warning only: it never blocks the query nor restricts permissions.

## ÁMBITO, AJUSTES Y COMPONENTES COMPARTIDOS (permanent, 2026-09-17)
47. **SUPERSEDED by rule 86** (Hospital / Corporativo = afiliación de SAP, no alcance configurable). Texto original: ROL ≠ ÁMBITO. The role answers «qué puede hacer»; the ámbito organizacional answers «sobre qué hospital, dirección o unidad». Both are approved by US-01 (scope-aware Home, own hospital / own dirección / consolidado, `users.json › hospital / direccion`, role scope «Consolidado»). UI copy always explains the difference in client language — never RBAC jargon.
48. **PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED by rule 86** (ya no se derivan opciones de ámbito asignable; el principio de no inventar catálogos sigue vigente). **NEVER INVENT AUTHORITATIVE CATALOGS** (organizational units, roles, incompatible pairs, statuses) for prototype convenience.
